# Video Prompts — Continuous Chain (6-second clips)

242 clips × 6 s, built as one continuous movie. **Clip N starts on Image N and ends on Image N+1**, so every clip's end frame is the next clip's start frame. The images are the 242 single images from image_prompts.md. Clip 242 starts and ends on Image 242.

## Intro

### Clip 1 (Beats 1–2)
**[Script Segment Text]** “Today we're looking at people who got tired of waiting for justice and decided to deliver it themselves.”

**Duration:** 6s · **Start frame:** Image 1 · **End frame:** Image 2

**Video Prompt:** 6-second continuous image-to-video shot from the start frame (Image 1) to the end frame (Image 2). No hard cuts, no jump cuts: it must feel like one unbroken movie. Action order: 1. 0–2s — Narrator stares at camera and taps the gavel impatiently (from the start frame (Image 1); camera: gentle slow zoom-in) 2. 2–4s — Narrator throws the book over his shoulder (the shot evolves smoothly so that the monocled narrator tossing aside a law book and pulling on a comically oversized detective hat, a small scale of justice tipped over at his feet; camera: subtle handheld-style drift) 3. 4–6s — transition: the camera pushes into the narrator's monocle; the lens fills the frame and opens directly onto the end-frame scene, one continuous move. Land exactly on the end frame (Image 2): Empty 2000s Indian district courtroom with a wooden judge's bench, ceiling fans, dusty sunbeams through tall barred windows, rows of benches. Ambient motion: background stays perfectly flat and still; dust particles float in the light beams; light haze drifts. Animation: 2D cutout-style limited animation: oversized plain-white caricature heads stay on-model with expressive eyebrow, eye and mouth changes; bare solid-black stick bodies and noodle limbs bend fluidly; smooth, cinematic pacing. Mood progression: deadpan, dry → mischievous → tense, foreboding. Keep every character with a plain white face on a bare solid-black stick body with no clothing, identical designs across both frames, consistent lighting direction, no morphing faces, no extra characters, no text changes. Style: 2D flat cel-shaded cartoon, adult-animation style, every character has a plain white face and head with only light grey shading (no skin tones), oversized highly detailed caricature heads on bare solid-black stick-figure bodies with thick noodle limbs and NO clothing on the bodies (only hats, wigs, hair, glasses and handheld props), bold clean black outlines, muted earthy palette with warm browns and dusty teal skies, red reserved for blood, detailed period props and painted backgrounds, exaggerated expressive faces, soft vignette, 16:9.
